Fallback ke Apps Script HANYA jika GitHub Public API terkena rate-limit (403/429) try { const rGas = await fetch(GSHEET_WEBHOOK_URL, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'text/plain;charset=utf-8' }, body: JSON.stringify({ action: 'check_github_web_404', username: username }) }); } } } catch (e) {} return { status: 'unknown' }; } async function runBulkGithubPatCheck() { const rawText = (document.getElementById('ghPatTokensInput')?.value || '').trim(); if (!rawText) { return showToast('⚠️ Paste minimal 1 PAT token terlebih dahulu!'); ${_esc(item.masked)} `; } resBody.appendChild(rowEl); sumBadge.innerText = `✅ ${activeCnt} | 🟣 ${flaggedCnt} | ❌ ${invalidCnt}`; listCnt.innerText = `${i + 1}/${tokens.length}`; transferBtn.innerText = `🚀 Lanjut Cek Umur & Link (${activeCnt} Akun Aktif) ➔`; if (i < tokens.length - 1 && !_ghPatAbortFlag) { // Jeda ultra-safe acak 2.5 - 4.5 detik antar token untuk menghindari secondary rate limit const jitterMs = 2500 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 2000); await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, jitterMs)); } } if (!_ghPatAbortFlag) { progLbl.innerText = `📤 Menyinkronkan hasil ke Google Sheet...`; } // Sync 1x bersih setelah seluruh batch selesai (tidak mengunci Apps Script di tengah jalan) await syncTab1DirectToPatAgeAudit_(); await queueRealtimePatAutoSync(true); if (!_ghPatAbortFlag) { progLbl.innerText = `✅ Selesai! ${activeCnt} Aktif • ${flaggedCnt} Flagged • ${invalidCnt} Invalid/403 • ☁️ Synced`; } btn.disabled = false; btn.innerText = '🛡️ Cek Ultra-Safe (40/Batch • 3.5s)'; if (stopBtn) stopBtn.style.display = 'none'; showToast(`✅ Selesai! ${activeCnt} Aktif, ${flaggedCnt} Flagged/404, ${invalidCnt} Invalid/403!`); } function transferActivePatsToAgeTab() { const activeItems = _ghPatCheckerResults.filter(r => r.active && r.username); if (activeItems.length === 0) { return showToast('⚠️ Tidak ada akun ACTIVE untuk dicek umurnya!'); } const usernamesText = activeItems.map(r => r.username).join('\n'); switchGhDiagnosticTab('age'); const ageInput = document.getElementById('ghAgeUsernamesInput'); if (ageInput) { ageInput.value = usernamesText; } runBulkGithubAgeCheck(); } function copyPatCheckerOutput(mode) { if (!_ghPatCheckerResults || _ghPatCheckerResults.length === 0) { return showToast('⚠️ Belum ada hasil pengecekan token!'); } let textOut = ''; if (mode === 'tokens') { const act = _ghPatCheckerResults.filter(r => r.active); if (act.length === 0) return showToast('⚠️ Tidak ada token aktif!'); textOut = act.map(r => r.token).join('\n'); } else if (mode === 'users') { const act = _ghPatCheckerResults.filter(r => r.active); if (act.length === 0) return showToast('⚠️ Tidak ada username aktif!'); textOut = act.map(r => r.username).join('\n'); } else { textOut = _ghPatCheckerResults.map(r => { const idxPad = String(r.index).padEnd(3, ' '); if (r.active) { return `${idxPad}. [ ACTIVE ] Token: ${r.masked} -> User: ${r.username.padEnd(18, ' ')} (Verified Public)`; } if (r.flagged) { return `${idxPad}. [ FLAGGED ] Token: ${r.masked} -> User: ${(r.username || '-').padEnd(18, ' ')} (Shadowbanned/404)`; } if (r.isError403) { return `${idxPad}. [ ERROR ] Token: ${r.masked} -> ${r.message}`; } return `${idxPad}. [ INVALID ] Token: ${r.masked} -> Message: ${r.message}`; }).join('\n'); } navigator.clipboard.writeText(textOut); showToast(`📋 Berhasil menyalin (${mode.toUpperCase()})!`); } function formatGithubCreatedWib(isoString) { if (!isoString) return 'Unknown Date'; const dt = new Date(isoString); if (isNaN(dt.getTime())) return isoString; const days = ['Sunday', 'Monday', 'Tuesday', 'Wednesday', 'Thursday', 'Friday', 'Saturday']; const months = ['Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'May', 'Jun', 'Jul', 'Aug', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dec']; const wibMs = dt.getTime() + (7 * 60 * 60 * 1000); const w = new Date(wibMs); const dayName = days[w.getUTCDay()]; const dd = String(w.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, '0'); const mmm = months[w.getUTCMonth()]; const yyyy = w.getUTCFullYear(); const hh = String(w.getUTCHours()).padStart(2, '0'); const mm = String(w.getUTCMinutes()).padStart(2, '0'); const ss = String(w.getUTCSeconds()).padStart(2, '0'); return `${dayName}, ${dd} ${mmm} ${yyyy} • ${hh}:${mm}:${ss} WIB`; } async function runBulkGithubAgeCheck() { const rawText = (document.getElementById('ghAgeUsernamesInput')?.value || '').trim(); if (!rawText) { return showToast('⚠️ Please paste at least 1 GitHub username!'); } const rawLines = rawText.split(/[\r\n,]+/).map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean); const parsedUsernames = []; for (const line of rawLines) { const logUserMatch = line.match(/\bUser:\s*([a-zA-Z0-9-]+)\b/i); if (logUserMatch && logUserMatch[1]) { parsedUsernames.push(logUserMatch[1].trim()); continue; } if (/\[\s*INVALID\s*\]/i.test(line) || /Bad credentials/i.test(line) || /\bToken:\s*ghp_/i.test(line)) { continue; } let cleanedLine = line.replace(/^\d+[\.\)\-:\s]+/, '').trim(); if (cleanedLine.includes('|')) cleanedLine = cleanedLine.split('|')[0].trim(); for (const tok of cleanedLine.split(/[\s\t]+/)) { const u = tok.replace(/^@/, '').replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9-]/g, '').trim(); } else { try { const livePool = activeTokens.filter(t => !deadTokensSet.has(t)); let res = null; if (livePool.length > 0) { let localIdx = (Math.floor(i / 50) + failSkipOffset) % livePool.length; for (let attempt = 0; function scbEsc(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/&/g, '&').replace(//g, '>').replace(/"/g, '"'); } function getPcBridgeCfg() { const uNew = (localStorage.getItem('xb_pc_bridge_url') || '').trim().replace(/\/+$/, ''); const kNew = (localStorage.getItem('xb_pc_bridge_key') || '').trim(); } function scbGetActiveTokens() { return Array.from(new Set([...Array.from(_scbState.selectedPats), ...scbExtractTokens(_scbState.pastedRaw)])); } async function scbLoadAuditPool(force = false) { if (_scbState.auditLoading || (!force && _scbState.auditPool.length > 0)) return; const d = await res.json(); const map = new Map(); if (d && d.result && Array.isArray(d.items)) { d.items.forEach((it, idx) => { const tok = String(it.patToken || '').trim(); if (tok.length >= 20 && !map.has(tok)) { map.set(tok, { pool_no: it.pool_no || (idx + 1), username: String(it.username || 'unknown').replace(/^@/, ''), year: String(it.year || 'Other'), patToken: tok }); } }); } if (typeof allLiveAccountsFlat !== 'undefined' && Array.isArray(allLiveAccountsFlat)) { allLiveAccountsFlat.forEach(acc => { const tok = String(acc.pat || '').trim(); if (tok.length >= 20 && tok !== '-' && !map.has(tok)) { map.set(tok, { pool_no: map.size + 1, username: String(acc.username || 'unknown').replace(/^@/, ''), year: String(acc.year || acc.category || 'Sheet1'), patToken: tok }); } function scbStopCdTicker() { if (_scbState.cdTimer) { clearInterval(_scbState.cdTimer); _scbState.cdTimer = null; } _scbState.cdUntil = 0; _scbState.cdTotal = 0; _scbState.cdLabel = ''; const box = document.getElementById('scbCdBanner'); ('l' + Math.min(4, Math.max(1, Math.ceil(rnd() * 4)))) : ''; html += ``; } return html; } function scbFormatLogCardHtml(lg) { let cleanMsg = String(lg.msg || '').trim().replace(/\.{2,}$/, '').trim(); let rightStage = ''; const m = cleanMsg.match(/\[([1-4]\/4)\]/); if (m) { rightStage = `[${m[1]}]`; cleanMsg = cleanMsg.replace(m[0], '').replace(/\s{2,}/g, ' ').trim(); } else if (lg.isDone) { rightStage = '[4/4]'; } const tag = String(lg.tag || '[PC]').toUpperCase(); const isCd = /COOLDOWN|WAIT/i.test(tag); const hdrCol = lg.isErr ? `; } function scbPatchMonitorDom() { const card = document.getElementById('scbMonCard'); if (!card) return scbRenderBody(); const t = _scbState.telemetry || { status: 'IDLE', feature_label: '', mission_id: '', stage: 0, stage_label: 'WAITING FOR MISSION QUEUE...', pct: 0, items: [], logs: [] }; const pct = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Number(t.pct || 0))); const st = String(t.status || 'IDLE').toUpperCase(); const curModObj = _SCB_MODULES.find(m => m.id === (t.feature || _scbState.mod)); const jobTitle = t.feature_label || (curModObj ? `${curModObj.ico} ${curModObj.title}` : '🛰️ STEALTH COMMAND'); const jobSub = t.mission_id ? `${t.mission_id} • ${st} (${pct}%)` : 'STANDBY • READY FOR MISSION'; const jtEl = document.getElementById('scbMonJobTitle'); const jsEl = document.getElementById('scbMonJobSub'); const stEl = document.getElementById('scbMonStageTxt'); const pfEl = document.getElementById('scbMonProgFill'); const sgEl = document.getElementById('scbMonStagesRow'); const itEl = document.getElementById('scbMonItemsList'); const lbEl = document.getElementById('scbLogBox'); if (jtEl) jtEl.innerText = jobTitle.toUpperCase(); if (jsEl) { jsEl.innerText = jobSub; jsEl.style.color = st === 'COMPLETED' ? `; }).join(''); } let _patSoundEnabled = localStorage.getItem('xb_pat_sound_on') !== 'false'; let _patAudioCtx = null; let _patMasterGain = null; let _lastSpokenStageKey = ''; function ensurePatAudioEngine() { const AudioCtx = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext; SCOPES & EXPIRATION'; } else if (/\[4\/4\]|Generating|Clicking Generate|Sheet1/i.test(stepText)) { if (!pctMatch) stepSubPct = 0.94; activeStage = 4; stageTitleCenter = '4. TOKEN DISPATCH & SHEET1'; } const pct = isStoppedByAdmin ? '🛑 BATCH STOPPED BY ADMIN' : '✅ 4. (lResult ^ 0xC0000000 ^ lX8 ^ lY8) : (lResult ^ 0x40000000 ^ lX8 ^ lY8); return (lResult ^ lX8 ^ lY8); } function F(x, y, z) { return (x & y) | ((~x) & z); } function G(x, y, z) { return (x & z) | (y & (~z)); } function H(x, y, z) { return (x ^ y ^ z); } function I(x, y, z) { return (y ^ (x | (~z))); } function FF(a, b, c, d, x, s, ac) { a = au(a, au(au(F(b, c, d), x), ac)); return au(rl(a, s), b); } function GG(a, b, c, d, x, s, ac) { a = au(a, au(au(G(b, c, d), x), ac)); return au(rl(a, s), b); } function HH(a, b, c, d, x, s, ac) { a = au(a, au(au(H(b, c, d), x), ac)); return au(rl(a, s), b); } function II(a, b, c, d, x, s, ac) { a = au(a, au(au(I(b, c, d), x), ac)); return au(rl(a, s), b); } function cwa(str) { let lWordCount; const lMessageLength = str.length; const lNumberOfWords_temp1 = lMessageLength + 8; const lNumberOfWords_temp2 = (lNumberOfWords_temp1 - (lNumberOfWords_temp1 % 64)) / 64; const lNumberOfWords = (lNumberOfWords_temp2 + 1) * 16; const lWordArray = Array(lNumberOfWords - 1); let lBytePosition = 0, lByteCount = 0; while (lByteCount < lMessageLength) { lWordCount = (lByteCount - (lByteCount % 4)) / 4; lBytePosition = (lByteCount % 4) * 8; lWordArray[lWordCount] = (lWordArray[lWordCount] | (str.charCodeAt(lByteCount) << lBytePosition)); lByteCount++; } lWordCount = (lByteCount - (lByteCount % 4)) / 4; lBytePosition = (lByteCount % 4) * 8; lWordArray[lWordCount] = lWordArray[lWordCount] | (0x80 << lBytePosition); lWordArray[lNumberOfWords - 2] = lMessageLength << 3; lWordArray[lNumberOfWords - 1] = lMessageLength >>> 29; return lWordArray; } function wth(lValue) { let WordToHexValue = "", WordToHexValue_temp = "", lByte, lCount; for (lCount = 0; lCount <= 3; lCount++) { lByte = (lValue >>> (lCount * 8)) & 255; WordToHexValue_temp = "0" + lByte.toString(16); WordToHexValue = WordToHexValue + WordToHexValue_temp.substr(WordToHexValue_temp.length - 2, 2); } return WordToHexValue; } const x = cwa(string); let a = 0x67452301, b = 0xEFCDAB89, c = 0x98BADCFE, d = 0x10325476; for (let k = 0; k < x.length; k += 16) { const AA = a, BB = b, CC = c, DD = d; a = FF(a, b, c, d, x[k + 0], 7, 0xD76AA478); d = FF(d, a, b, c, x[k + 1], 12, 0xE8C7B756); c = FF(c, d, a, b, x[k + 2], 17, 0x242070DB); a = FF(a, b, c, d, x[k + 8], 7, 0x698098D8); d = FF(d, a, b, c, x[k + 9], 12, 0x8B44F7AF); c = FF(c, d, a, b, x[k + 10], 17, 0xFFFF5BB1); b = FF(b, c, d, a, x[k + 11], 22, 0x895CD7BE); a = FF(a, b, c, d, x[k + 12], 7, 0x6B901122); d = FF(d, a, b, c, x[k + 13], 12, 0xFD987193); c = FF(c, d, a, b, x[k + 14], 17, 0xA679438E); b = FF(b, c, d, a, x[k + 15], 22, 0x49B40821); a = GG(a, b, c, d, x[k + 1], 5, 0xF61E2562); d = GG(d, a, b, c, x[k + 6], 9, 0xC040B340); if (!/^\d{6}$/.test(cleanPin)) return false; const enteredHash = md5Hash(cleanPin); const botHash = (localStorage.getItem('xb_bot_pin_hash') || '').trim().toLowerCase(); const localPlainPin = (localStorage.getItem('xb_user_pin') || '').trim(); Tap Refresh to retry.'; } finally { st.loadingPlat = ''; if (activeTab === 'boost' && window._bstPlatform === platKey) { renderViewport(); } } } function computeSmmSelectedTotals(platKey) { const st = window._smmState; const svc = st.selectedSvcByPlat[platKey] || null; const fx = Math.max(14000, Number(liveUsdtIdrRate || 18195)); if (!svc) return { svc: null, qty: 0, totalIdr: 0, totalUsdt: 0, rate1kUsdt: 0, belowMin: true, overMax: false }; const qty = Math.max(0, parseInt(st.qty, 10) || 0); const totalIdr = Math.round((qty / 1000) * Number(svc.sell_1k_idr || 0)); const totalUsdt = Number((totalIdr / fx).toFixed(2)); const rate1kUsdt = Number((Number(svc.sell_1k_idr || 0) / fx).toFixed(2)); const belowMin = qty < svc.min || totalUsdt < 1.00; const overMax = qty > svc.max; return { svc, qty, totalIdr, totalUsdt, rate1kUsdt, belowMin, overMax }; } function updateSmmLivePriceDom(platKey) { const calc = computeSmmSelectedTotals(platKey); const totEl = document.getElementById('smmLiveTotalVal'); const idrSub = document.getElementById('smmLiveTotalSub'); const btn = document.getElementById('smmSubmitOrderBtn'); if (!calc.svc) return; if (totEl) totEl.innerText = fmtPrice(calc.totalUsdt); if (idrSub) { idrSub.innerText = currentCurrency === 'IDR' ? `; root.classList.add('open'); setTimeout(() => { const el = document.getElementById('smmPinInp'); if (el) el.focus(); }, 60); } async function executeSmmConfirmedOrder() { const st = window._smmState; const ord = st.pendingPinOrd; if (!ord || st.submitting) return; const inp = document.getElementById('smmPinInp'); const errEl = document.getElementById('smmPinErr'); const btn = document.getElementById('smmConfirmPayBtn'); const pin = String(inp ? inp.value : '').trim(); if (!/^\d{6}$/.test(pin)) { if (errEl) errEl.innerText = 'PIN must be exactly 6 numeric digits.'; return; } const pinOk = (typeof isPinValidWithBot === 'function') ? isPinValidWithBot(pin) : (xbExMd5(pin) === String(localStorage.getItem('xb_bot_pin_hash') || '').toLowerCase()); if (!pinOk) { if (errEl) errEl.innerText = 'Incorrect 6-digit Security PIN.'; return; } if (ord.totalUsdt > userBalanceUsdt) { if (errEl) errEl.innerText = 'Insufficient wallet balance.'; return; } st.submitting = true; if (btn) { btn.disabled = true; btn.innerText = '⏳ Dispatching to Server...'; } if (errEl) errEl.innerText = ''; const invId = 'INV-SMM-' + Math.floor(10000 + Math.random() * 89999); const dispUser = currentUsername.startsWith('@') ? currentUsername : `@${currentUsername}`; try { // 1. Place order to IndoSMM FIRST (if rejected, balance is NOT deducted) const res = await fetch(GSHEET_WEBHOOK_URL, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'text/plain;charset=utf-8' }, body: JSON.stringify({ action: 'smm_indosmm', sub_action: 'place_order', invoice_id: invId, buyer_uid: String(currentUserId), buyer_username: dispUser, platform: ord.platKey, service_id: ord.svc.id, service_name: ord.svc.name, link: ord.target, quantity: ord.qty, price_usd: ord.totalUsdt, idr_rate: Math.round(liveUsdtIdrRate || 18195) }) }); const json = await res.json(); if (!json || !json.result || !json.smm_order_id) { if (errEl) errEl.innerText = (json && json.error) ? json.error : 'Server rejected order. Balance NOT deducted.'; if (btn) { btn.disabled = false; btn.innerText = '✅ Confirm & Dispatch'; } st.submitting = false; return; } // 2. IN-BROWSER 2FA OTP & DIAGNOSTIC TOOLS // ========================================================= let otpIntervalId = null; function render2faTool(vp) { if (otpIntervalId) clearInterval(otpIntervalId); vp.innerHTML = `
LIVE 30-SECOND TOTP AUTHENTICATOR

🔑 Live 2FA OTP Code Generator

Paste your Base32 2FA Secret Key to get a 6-digit login code
6-DIGIT OTP CODE (TAP TO COPY)
------
Refreshing in 30s
`; } function base32Decode(str) { const alphabet = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ234567"; const clean = str.toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z2-7]/g, ""); let bits = ""; for (let i = 0; i < clean.length; i++) bits += alphabet.indexOf(clean[i]).toString(2).padStart(5, "0"); const out = []; for (let i = 0; i + 8 <= bits.length; i += 8) out.push(parseInt(bits.substring(i, i + 8), 2)); return new Uint8Array(out); } async function generateTotpCode(secret) { const key = base32Decode(secret); if (!key.length) throw new Error("Invalid key"); const counter = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000 / 30); const buf = new ArrayBuffer(8); new DataView(buf).setUint32(4, counter, false); const cryptoKey = await crypto.subtle.importKey("raw", key, { name: "HMAC", hash: "SHA-1" }, false, ["sign"]); const hmac = new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.sign("HMAC", cryptoKey, buf)); const offset = hmac[hmac.length - 1] & 0x0f; const bin = ((hmac[offset] & 0x7f) << 24) | ((hmac[offset + 1] & 0xff) << 16) | ((hmac[offset + 2] & 0xff) << 8) | (hmac[offset + 3] & 0xff); return (bin % 1000000).toString().padStart(6, "0"); } async function start2faGenerator() { const s = document.getElementById('web2faSecret').value.trim(); if (!s) return showToast('Enter a valid 2FA Secret Key!'); document.getElementById('web2faBox').style.display = 'block'; async function updateOtp() { try { const code = await generateTotpCode(s); const rem = 30 - (Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) % 30); document.getElementById('web2faCode').innerText = code; document.getElementById('web2faTimer').innerText = `Expires in ${rem}s (Auto-Refreshing)`; } catch (e) { document.getElementById('web2faCode').innerText = 'INVALID'; } } await updateOtp(); if (otpIntervalId) clearInterval(otpIntervalId); otpIntervalId = setInterval(updateOtp, 1000); } function renderDiagnosticTools(vp) { vp.innerHTML = `

} document.getElementById('cartUserBal').innerText = fmtPrice(userBalanceUsdt); document.getElementById('cartModal').classList.add('active'); } function claimWalletVoucher() { const code = (document.getElementById('walletPromoInput')?.value || '').trim().toUpperCase(); } function sendPayloadToBot(action, extra = {}) { // Disabled tg.sendData() so the web store never closes or redirects console.log("[STANDALONE WEB ACTION]", action, extra); } function closeModal(id) { const el = document.getElementById(id); } } let toastTimeout = null; function showToast(msg) { const t = document.getElementById('toastBanner'); t.innerText = '⚡ ' + msg; t.classList.add('show'); clearTimeout(toastTimeout); toastTimeout = setTimeout(() => t.classList.remove('show'), 2600); } // ========================================================= // 📊 100% ENGLISH GLOBAL STORE SALES 3D TELEMETRY (2 3D CHARTS: ACCOUNTS SOLD & GITHUB SERVICES + DAILY, WEEKLY, YEARLY, ALL-TIME USDT) // ========================================================= let _storeAccChart3D = null; let _storeSvcChart3D = null; let _storeTelemetryRange = 'ALL'; // Isometric 3D Prism Bar Plugin + Value Labels Above 3D Bars const xbPrism3DPlugin = { id: 'xbPrism3DPlugin', afterDatasetsDraw(chart) { const ctx = chart.ctx; const dx = 6; const dy = -5; chart.data.datasets.forEach((dataset, dsIdx) => { const meta = chart.getDatasetMeta(dsIdx); if (meta.hidden || meta.type !== 'bar') return; meta.data.forEach((bar, index) => { const val = dataset.data[index]; if (!val || val <= 0) return; const x = bar.x; const y = bar.y; const base = bar.base; const w = bar.width; const left = x - w / 2; const right = x + w / 2; if (base - y <= 1) return; ctx.save(); // 1. 3D Top Bevel Face ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(left, y); ctx.lineTo(left + dx, y + dy); ctx.lineTo(right + dx, y + dy); ctx.lineTo(right, y); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fillStyle = dataset.top3dColor || '#a7f3d0'; ctx.fill(); // 2. 3D Right Extrusion Wall ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(right, y); ctx.lineTo(right + dx, y + dy); ctx.lineTo(right + dx, base + dy); ctx.lineTo(right, base); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fillStyle = dataset.side3dColor || '#047857'; ctx.fill(); // 3. Value Label on Top of 3D Roof ctx.font = '800 8px "JetBrains Mono", monospace'; ctx.fillStyle = dataset.labelColor || '#f8fafc'; ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.fillText(String(val), x + dx / 2, y + dy - 3); ctx.restore(); }); }); } }; function getGlobalStoreSalesData() { const rawTel = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('xb_gsheet_telemetry') || 'null'); const accLabels = ['11-13', '14-16', '17-19', '20-22', '23-25', '2026', 'Arctic', '6Badge+']; const svcLabels = ['Followers', 'Stars', 'Forks', 'Watchers']; let dailyUsdt = 0, weeklyUsdt = 0, yearlyUsdt = 0, allTimeUsdt = 0; let dailyTx = 0, weeklyTx = 0, yearlyTx = 0, allTimeTx = 0; let totalSoldAccs = 0, totalSoldAccsUsdt = 0; let totalBoostOrders = 0, totalBoostUsdt = 0; let accSoldBuckets = [0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0]; let accRevBuckets = [0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0]; let svcCounts = [0, 0, 0, 0]; let svcRevs = [0, 0, 0, 0]; if (rawTel && (Number(rawTel.allTimeUsdt) > 0 || Number(rawTel.totalSoldAccs) > 0 || Number(rawTel.totalBoostOrders) > 0)) { dailyUsdt = parseFloat(rawTel.dailyUsdt || 0); weeklyUsdt = parseFloat(rawTel.weeklyUsdt || 0); yearlyUsdt = parseFloat(rawTel.yearlyUsdt || 0); allTimeUsdt = parseFloat(rawTel.allTimeUsdt || 0); dailyTx = parseInt(rawTel.dailyTx || 0); weeklyTx = parseInt(rawTel.weeklyTx || 0); yearlyTx = parseInt(rawTel.yearlyTx || 0); allTimeTx = parseInt(rawTel.allTimeTx || (parseInt(rawTel.totalSoldAccs || 0) + parseInt(rawTel.totalBoostOrders || 0))); totalSoldAccs = parseInt(rawTel.totalSoldAccs || 0); totalBoostOrders = parseInt(rawTel.totalBoostOrders || 0); if (Array.isArray(rawTel.accSoldBuckets)) { rawTel.accSoldBuckets.forEach((v, i) => { if (i < 8) accSoldBuckets[i] = Number(v || 0); }); } if (Array.isArray(rawTel.accRevBuckets)) { rawTel.accRevBuckets.forEach((v, i) => { if (i < 8) accRevBuckets[i] = Number(v || 0); }); } if (Array.isArray(rawTel.svcCounts)) { rawTel.svcCounts.slice(0, 4).forEach((v, i) => { svcCounts[i] = Number(v || 0); }); } if (Array.isArray(rawTel.svcRevs)) { rawTel.svcRevs.slice(0, 4).forEach((v, i) => { svcRevs[i] = Number(v || 0); }); } totalSoldAccsUsdt = parseFloat(rawTel.totalSoldAccsUsdt || accRevBuckets.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0)); totalBoostUsdt = parseFloat(rawTel.totalBoostUsdt || svcRevs.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0)); } const scale = _storeTelemetryRange === 'DAY' ? (allTimeUsdt > 0 && dailyUsdt > 0 ? Math.max(0.08, dailyUsdt / allTimeUsdt) : 0.12) : (_storeTelemetryRange === 'WEEK' ? (allTimeUsdt > 0 && weeklyUsdt > 0 ? Math.max(0.22, weeklyUsdt / allTimeUsdt) : 0.35) : (_storeTelemetryRange === 'YEAR' ? (allTimeUsdt > 0 && yearlyUsdt > 0 ? Math.max(0.75, yearlyUsdt / allTimeUsdt) : 0.9) : 1.0)); const scaledAccSold = _storeTelemetryRange === 'ALL' ? accSoldBuckets : accSoldBuckets.map(v => Math.round(v * scale)); const scaledAccRev = _storeTelemetryRange === 'ALL' ? accRevBuckets : accRevBuckets.map(v => Math.round(v * scale)); const scaledSvcCnt = _storeTelemetryRange === 'ALL' ? svcCounts : svcCounts.map(v => Math.round(v * scale)); const scaledSvcRev = _storeTelemetryRange === 'ALL' ? svcRevs : svcRevs.map(v => Math.round(v * scale)); return { dailyUsdt, weeklyUsdt, yearlyUsdt, allTimeUsdt, dailyTx, weeklyTx, yearlyTx, allTimeTx, totalSoldAccs: _storeTelemetryRange === 'ALL' ? totalSoldAccs : scaledAccSold.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0), totalSoldAccsUsdt: _storeTelemetryRange === 'ALL' ? totalSoldAccsUsdt : scaledAccRev.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0), totalBoostOrders: _storeTelemetryRange === 'ALL' ? totalBoostOrders : scaledSvcCnt.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0), totalBoostUsdt: _storeTelemetryRange === 'ALL' ? totalBoostUsdt : scaledSvcRev.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0), accLabels, accSoldData: scaledAccSold, accRevData: scaledAccRev, svcLabels, svcCountData: scaledSvcCnt, svcRevData: scaledSvcRev }; } function switchStoreTelemetryRange(rangeKey) { _storeTelemetryRange = rangeKey; openStoreGlobal3DTelemetry(false); } async function refreshStoreGlobalTelemetryNow() { const btn = document.getElementById('btnRefreshStoreTelemetry'); if (btn) btn.innerText = '⏳'; await syncGoogleSheetInventory(false); openStoreGlobal3DTelemetry(false); showToast('📊 Real-time store sales telemetry synced from Google Sheet!'); } function openStoreGlobal3DTelemetry(autoFetchIfEmpty = true) { const modal = document.getElementById('paymentGatewayModal'); const body = document.getElementById('paymentGatewayBody'); modal.classList.add('active'); if (autoFetchIfEmpty && !localStorage.getItem('xb_gsheet_telemetry')) { syncGoogleSheetInventory(false).then(() => { if (modal.classList.contains('active')) openStoreGlobal3DTelemetry(false); }); } const st = getGlobalStoreSalesData(); const r = _storeTelemetryRange; body.style.padding = '11px 12px'; body.style.maxWidth = '540px'; body.innerHTML = `

$${st.weeklyUsdt.toFixed(2)}

YEARLY (1Y)
$${st.yearlyUsdt.toFixed(1)}
USDT • ${st.yearlyTx} TX

USDT • ${st.allTimeTx} TX

`; setTimeout(() => { if (!window.Chart) return; if (_storeAccChart3D) { try { _storeAccChart3D.destroy(); } catch (e) {} } if (_storeSvcChart3D) { try { _storeSvcChart3D.destroy(); } catch (e) {} } // RENDER CHART 1: 3D ACCOUNTS SOLD (100% ENGLISH LABELS) const elAcc = document.getElementById('xbStoreAccSoldCanvas3D'); grad1.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(2, 132, 199, 0.32)'); _storeAccChart3D = new Chart(ctx1, { type: 'bar', plugins: [xbPrism3DPlugin], data: { labels: st.accLabels, datasets: [ { type: 'line', label: 'Revenue ($ USDT)', data: st.accRevData, borderColor: '#fde047', backgroundColor: 'rgba(250, 204, 21, 0.14)', borderWidth: 1.8, pointRadius: 2.5, pointBackgroundColor: '#fde047', tension: 0.35, yAxisID: 'y1' }, { type: 'bar', label: 'Accounts Sold', data: st.accSoldData, backgroundColor: grad1, top3dColor: '#a7f3d0', side3dColor: '#047857', labelColor: '#ecfdf5', borderRadius: 3, barPercentage: 0.56, yAxisID: 'y' } ] }, options: { responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false, layout: { padding: { top: 12, right: 6, left: 0, bottom: 0 } }, plugins: { legend: { display: true, labels: { color: '#cbd5e1', font: { size: 7.5, weight: 'bold' }, boxWidth: 8, padding: 4 } } }, scales: { x: { ticks: { color: '#cbd5e1', font: { size: 7.5, weight: 'bold' } }, grid: { display: false } }, y: { beginAtZero: true, position: 'left', ticks: { color: '#34d399', font: { size: 7.5 }, maxTicksLimit: 4, precision: 0 }, grid: { color: 'rgba(148,163,184,0.1)' } }, y1: { beginAtZero: true, position: 'right', ticks: { color: '#fde047', font: { size: 7.5 }, maxTicksLimit: 4, callback: v => '$' + v }, grid: { display: false } } } } }); } // RENDER CHART 2: 3D GITHUB SERVICES SOLD (100% ENGLISH LABELS) const elSvc = document.getElementById('xbStoreSvcSoldCanvas3D'); if (elSvc) { const ctx2 = elSvc.getContext('2d'); const grad2 = ctx2.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, 105); grad2.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(232, 121, 249, 0.95)'); grad2.addColorStop(0.55, 'rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.82)'); grad2.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(79, 70, 229, 0.32)'); _storeSvcChart3D = new Chart(ctx2, { type: 'bar', plugins: [xbPrism3DPlugin], data: { labels: st.svcLabels, datasets: [ { type: 'line', label: 'Revenue ($ USDT)', data: st.svcRevData, borderColor: '#38bdf8', backgroundColor: 'rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.16)', fill: true, borderWidth: 1.8, pointRadius: 2.5, pointBackgroundColor: '#38bdf8', tension: 0.38, yAxisID: 'y1' }, { type: 'bar', label: 'Service Orders', data: st.svcCountData, backgroundColor: grad2, top3dColor: '#f5d0fe', side3dColor: '#7e22ce', labelColor: '#fae8ff', borderRadius: 3, barPercentage: 0.48, yAxisID: 'y' } ] }, options: { responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false, layout: { padding: { top: 12, right: 6, left: 0, bottom: 0 } }, plugins: { legend: { display: true, labels: { color: '#cbd5e1', font: { size: 7.5, weight: 'bold' }, boxWidth: 8, padding: 4 } } }, scales: { x: { ticks: { color: '#cbd5e1', font: { size: 8, weight: 'bold' } }, grid: { display: false } }, y: { beginAtZero: true, position: 'left', ticks: { color: '#e879f9', font: { size: 7.5 }, maxTicksLimit: 4, precision: 0 }, grid: { color: 'rgba(148,163,184,0.1)' } }, y1: { beginAtZero: true, position: 'right', ticks: { color: '#38bdf8', font: { size: 7.5 }, maxTicksLimit: 4, callback: v => '$' + v }, grid: { display: false } } } } }); } }, 60); } // ========================================================= // 💎 BENTO WEALTH — PERSONAL 3D ANALYTICS (3D DONUT + 3D AREA TREND) // ========================================================= window._xbBentoWealth = window._xbBentoWealth || { isOpen: false, tf: 'ALL', // 'DAY' | 'WEEK' | 'YEAR' | 'ALL' cur: 'USDT', // 'USDT' | 'IDR' activeSlice: -1, // -1 = all, 0 = Accounts, 1 = Boosts, 2 = Other hoverTrendIdx: -1, snapshot: null }; function closePersonalBentoWealthModal() { window._xbBentoWealth.isOpen = false; const modal = document.getElementById('paymentGatewayModal'); if (modal) modal.classList.remove('xb-bw-modal', 'xb-fullpage-modal', 'xb-cyber-telem-modal'); closeModal('paymentGatewayModal'); } function setBentoWealthTf(tfKey) { window._xbBentoWealth.tf = tfKey; window._xbBentoWealth.hoverTrendIdx = -1; renderPersonalBentoWealthView(); } function toggleBentoWealthCur() { window._xbBentoWealth.cur = window._xbBentoWealth.cur === 'USDT' ? 'IDR' : 'USDT'; renderPersonalBentoWealthView(); } function selectBentoWealthSlice(idx) { window._xbBentoWealth.activeSlice = (window._xbBentoWealth.activeSlice === idx) ? -1 : idx; renderPersonalBentoWealthView(); } function fmtBentoMoney(usdtVal, compactIdr = false) { const v = Number(usdtVal || 0); if (window._xbBentoWealth.cur === 'IDR') { const idr = Math.round(v * (typeof getCyberTelemIdrRate === 'function' ? getCyberTelemIdrRate() : liveUsdtIdrRate)); if (compactIdr) { if (idr >= 1e9) return 'Rp ' + (idr / 1e9).toFixed(2) + 'M'; if (idr >= 1e6) return 'Rp ' + (idr / 1e6).toFixed(2) + 'Jt'; if (idr >= 1e3) return 'Rp ' + Math.round(idr / 1e3) + 'Rb'; } return 'Rp ' + idr.toLocaleString('id-ID'); } return '$' + v.toLocaleString('en-US', { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 }); } function buildPersonalBentoSnapshot(tfKey) { const history = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('xb_tx_history') || '[]'); const vault = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('xb_purchased_vault') || '[]'); const now = new Date(); const nowMs = now.getTime(); const curYear = now.getFullYear(); const parseTxTs = (dateStr) => { if (!dateStr) return nowMs; const s = String(dateStr).trim(); let ts = Date.parse(s); if (isNaN(ts)) ts = Date.parse(`${s}, ${curYear}`); if (!isNaN(ts) && ts > nowMs + 86400000) { const prevYr = Date.parse(`${s}, ${curYear - 1}`); if (!isNaN(prevYr)) ts = prevYr; } return isNaN(ts) ? nowMs : ts; }; const cutoffMs = tfKey === 'DAY' ? (nowMs - 86400000) : (tfKey === 'WEEK' ? (nowMs - 7 * 86400000) : (tfKey === 'YEAR' ? new Date(curYear, 0, 1).getTime() : 0)); let accSpend = 0, boostSpend = 0, otherSpend = 0; let accOrders = 0, boostOrders = 0, otherOrders = 0; const filteredTxs = []; const svcCounts = { Followers: 0, Stars: 0, Forks: 0, Watchers: 0 }; history.forEach(tx => { if (!tx || tx.type !== 'OUT') return; if (typeof isP2pTransferTx === 'function' && isP2pTransferTx(tx)) return; const amt = parseFloat(tx.usdt || 0); if (isNaN(amt) || amt <= 0) return; const ts = parseTxTs(tx.date); if (cutoffMs > 0 && ts < cutoffMs) return; const isBoost = typeof isBoostServiceOrderTx === 'function' && isBoostServiceOrderTx(tx); const tLow = String(tx.title || '').toLowerCase(); let kind = 'other'; if (isBoost) { kind = 'boost'; boostSpend += amt; boostOrders++; if (tLow.includes('star')) svcCounts.Stars++; else if (tLow.includes('fork')) svcCounts.Forks++; else if (tLow.includes('watch')) svcCounts.Watchers++; else svcCounts.Followers++; } else if (tLow.includes('github') || tLow.includes('vault') || tLow.includes('arctic') || tLow.includes('badge') || tLow.includes('account')) { kind = 'acc'; accSpend += amt; accOrders++; } else { otherSpend += amt; otherOrders++; } filteredTxs.push({ ts, amt, kind, title: tx.title || 'Order' }); }); // Count top purchased Vault tier const vaultCatCnt = {}; let vaultTotalVal = 0; vault.forEach(v => { if (!v) return; vaultTotalVal += parseFloat(v.priceUsdt || 0) || 0; const c = String(v.category || '2026'); vaultCatCnt[c] = (vaultCatCnt[c] || 0) + 1; }); const topVaultEntry = Object.entries(vaultCatCnt).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])[0]; const topSvcEntry = Object.entries(svcCounts).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])[0]; // Build 6-point Trend Series matching active timeframe const trendPoints = []; const mos = ['Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'May', 'Jun', 'Jul', 'Aug', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dec']; if (tfKey === 'DAY') { for (let i = 5; i >= 0; i--) { const bEnd = nowMs - i * 4 * 3600000; const bStart = bEnd - 4 * 3600000; const d = new Date(bEnd); const lbl = String(d.getHours()).padStart(2, '0') + ':00'; const match = filteredTxs.filter(t => t.ts > bStart && t.ts <= bEnd); trendPoints.push({ label: lbl, val: match.reduce((s, x) => s + x.amt, 0), tx: match.length }); } } else if (tfKey === 'WEEK') { const days = ['Sun', 'Mon', 'Tue', 'Wed', 'Thu', 'Fri', 'Sat']; for (let i = 6; i >= 0; i--) { const d = new Date(nowMs - i * 86400000); const bStart = new Date(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth(), d.getDate()).getTime(); const bEnd = bStart + 86400000; const match = filteredTxs.filter(t => t.ts >= bStart && t.ts < bEnd); trendPoints.push({ label: days[d.getDay()], val: match.reduce((s, x) => s + x.amt, 0), tx: match.length }); } } else { for (let i = 5; i >= 0; i--) { const d = new Date(curYear, now.getMonth() - i, 1); const bStart = d.getTime(); const bEnd = new Date(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth() + 1, 1).getTime(); const match = filteredTxs.filter(t => t.ts >= bStart && t.ts < bEnd); trendPoints.push({ label: mos[d.getMonth()], val: match.reduce((s, x) => s + x.amt, 0), tx: match.length }); } } const totalSpend = accSpend + boostSpend + otherSpend; const totalOrders = accOrders + boostOrders + otherOrders; const avgOrder = totalOrders > 0 ? totalSpend / totalOrders : 0; const slices = [ { key: 'acc', label: 'Aged Accounts', val: accSpend, cnt: accOrders, topCol: '#38bdf8', sideCol: '#0369a1', glow: 'rgba(56,189,248,0.45)' }, { key: 'boost', label: 'GitHub Boost', val: boostSpend, cnt: boostOrders, topCol: '#a855f7', sideCol: '#6b21a8', glow: 'rgba(168,85,247,0.45)' }, { key: 'other', label: 'Other Store', val: otherSpend, cnt: otherOrders, topCol: '#10b981', sideCol: '#047857', glow: 'rgba(16,185,129,0.45)' } ]; return { totalSpend, totalOrders, avgOrder, accSpend, boostSpend, otherSpend, accOrders, boostOrders, otherOrders, vaultCount: vault.length, vaultTotalVal, topVault: topVaultEntry ? `GitHub ${topVaultEntry[0]} (${topVaultEntry[1]}x)` : 'None yet', topSvc: (topSvcEntry && topSvcEntry[1] > 0) ? `${topSvcEntry[0]} (${topSvcEntry[1]}x)` : 'None yet', slices, trendPoints }; } function drawBento3DDonutCanvas(snap) { const c = document.getElementById('xbBwDonutCanvas'); if (!c) return; const rect = c.getBoundingClientRect(); const w = Math.max(150, Math.floor(rect.width || 180)); const h = Math.max(120, Math.floor(rect.height || 140)); const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1; c.width = w * dpr; c.height = h * dpr; const ctx = c.getContext('2d'); ctx.scale(dpr, dpr); ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h); const isPlat = document.documentElement.getAttribute('data-theme') === 'platinum'; const cx = w / 2; const cy = h / 2 - 4; const rx = Math.min(w * 0.41, 62); const ry = rx * 0.64; const innerRatio = 0.56; const depth = 13; const total = snap.slices.reduce((s, x) => s + x.val, 0); const activeIdx = window._xbBentoWealth.activeSlice; // Soft 3D base shadow ctx.save(); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(cx, cy + depth + 6, rx * 0.96, ry * 0.96, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fillStyle = isPlat ? 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.12)' : 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.52)'; ctx.fill(); ctx.restore(); if (total <= 0.001) { // Sleek empty 3D ring for (let z = depth; z >= 0; z--) { ctx.save(); ctx.translate(cx, cy + z); ctx.scale(1, ry / rx); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, rx, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.arc(0, 0, rx * innerRatio, 0, Math.PI * 2, true); ctx.fillStyle = z === 0 ? (isPlat ? '#cbd5e1' : '#1e293b') : (isPlat ? '#94a3b8' : '#0f172a'); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore(); } ctx.fillStyle = isPlat ? '#475569' : '#94a3b8'; ctx.font = '800 10px "JetBrains Mono", monospace'; ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle'; ctx.fillText('0% SPENT', cx, cy); c._sliceMeta = []; return; } // Prepare slice angles let startAng = -Math.PI / 2; const segs = []; snap.slices.forEach((sl, idx) => { if (sl.val <= 0) return; const sweep = (sl.val / total) * Math.PI * 2; const endAng = startAng + sweep; const midAng = (startAng + endAng) / 2; segs.push({ idx, sl, startAng, endAng, midAng, pct: Math.round((sl.val / total) * 100) }); startAng = endAng; }); c._sliceMeta = { cx, cy, rx, ry, innerRatio, segs }; // Draw 3D extrusion layers from bottom to top for (let z = depth; z >= 0; z--) { segs.forEach(seg => { const isPop = activeIdx === seg.idx; const lift = isPop ? 7 : 0; const ox = Math.cos(seg.midAng) * lift; const oy = Math.sin(seg.midAng) * lift * (ry / rx) - (isPop ? 3 : 0); ctx.save(); ctx.translate(cx + ox, cy + oy + z); ctx.scale(1, ry / rx); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, rx, seg.startAng, seg.endAng, false); ctx.arc(0, 0, rx * innerRatio, seg.endAng, seg.startAng, true); ctx.closePath(); if (z === 0) { ctx.fillStyle = seg.sl.topCol; if (isPop) { ctx.shadowColor = seg.sl.glow; ctx.shadowBlur = 12; } ctx.fill(); ctx.lineWidth = isPop ? 1.8 : 0.9; ctx.strokeStyle = isPop ? '#ffffff' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.28)'; ctx.stroke(); } else { ctx.fillStyle = seg.sl.sideCol; ctx.fill(); } ctx.restore(); }); } // Center label inside 3D hole const focused = segs.find(s => s.idx === activeIdx); const ctrTop = focused ? `${focused.pct}%` : '100%'; const ctrSub = focused ? focused.sl.label.split(' ')[0].toUpperCase() : 'TOTAL'; ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle'; ctx.fillStyle = isPlat ? '#0f172a' : '#f8fafc'; ctx.font = '800 13px "JetBrains Mono", monospace'; ctx.fillText(ctrTop, cx, cy - 4); ctx.fillStyle = focused ? focused.sl.topCol : (isPlat ? '#475569' : '#94a3b8'); ctx.font = '700 8px "JetBrains Mono", monospace'; ctx.fillText(ctrSub, cx, cy + 9); } function drawBento3DTrendCanvas(snap) { const c = document.getElementById('xbBwTrendCanvas'); if (!c) return; const rect = c.getBoundingClientRect(); const w = Math.max(220, Math.floor(rect.width || 320)); const h = Math.max(110, Math.floor(rect.height || 130)); const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1; c.width = w * dpr; c.height = h * dpr; const ctx = c.getContext('2d'); ctx.scale(dpr, dpr); ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h); const isPlat = document.documentElement.getAttribute('data-theme') === 'platinum'; const ptsData = snap.trendPoints || []; const padL = 34, padR = 14, padT = 16, padB = 22; const pw = Math.max(60, w - padL - padR); const ph = Math.max(40, h - padT - padB); const maxVal = Math.max(10, ...ptsData.map(p => p.val * 1.22)); const hoverIdx = window._xbBentoWealth.hoverTrendIdx; // Grid & Y labels ctx.font = '700 8px "JetBrains Mono", monospace'; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle'; [0, 0.5, 1].forEach(fr => { const y = padT + ph * (1 - fr); ctx.strokeStyle = isPlat ? 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.08)' : 'rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.14)'; ctx.lineWidth = 1; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(padL, y); ctx.lineTo(w - padR, y); ctx.stroke(); const tickVal = maxVal * fr; ctx.fillStyle = isPlat ? '#64748b' : '#94a3b8'; ctx.textAlign = 'right'; ctx.fillText(tickVal >= 1000 ? '$' + (tickVal / 1000).toFixed(1) + 'k' : '$' + Math.round(tickVal), padL - 5, y); }); const n = Math.max(1, ptsData.length); const step = n > 1 ? pw / (n - 1) : pw; const coords = ptsData.map((p, i) => ({ x: padL + (n > 1 ? i * step : pw / 2), y: padT + ph - (Math.min(maxVal, p.val) / maxVal) * ph, data: p })); c._trendCoords = coords; // 3D Depth Shadow Ribbon (+4px offset) if (coords.length > 1) { ctx.save(); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(coords[0].x + 4, coords[0].y + 5); for (let i = 0; i < coords.length - 1; i++) { const mx = (coords[i].x + coords[i + 1].x) / 2 + 4; ctx.bezierCurveTo(mx, coords[i].y + 5, mx, coords[i + 1].y + 5, coords[i + 1].x + 4, coords[i + 1].y + 5); } ctx.lineTo(coords[coords.length - 1].x + 4, padT + ph); ctx.lineTo(coords[0].x + 4, padT + ph); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fillStyle = isPlat ? 'rgba(124, 58, 237, 0.12)' : 'rgba(124, 58, 237, 0.22)'; ctx.fill(); ctx.restore(); // Main Glowing Area Fill const grad = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, padT, 0, padT + ph); grad.addColorStop(0, isPlat ? 'rgba(37, 99, 235, 0.32)' : 'rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.42)'); grad.addColorStop(0.65, isPlat ? 'rgba(124, 58, 237, 0.12)' : 'rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.18)'); grad.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0)'); ctx.save(); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(coords[0].x, coords[0].y); for (let i = 0; i < coords.length - 1; i++) { const mx = (coords[i].x + coords[i + 1].x) / 2; ctx.bezierCurveTo(mx, coords[i].y, mx, coords[i + 1].y, coords[i + 1].x, coords[i + 1].y); } ctx.lineTo(coords[coords.length - 1].x, padT + ph); ctx.lineTo(coords[0].x, padT + ph); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fillStyle = grad; ctx.fill(); // Top Neon 3D Spline ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(coords[0].x, coords[0].y); for (let i = 0; i < coords.length - 1; i++) { const mx = (coords[i].x + coords[i + 1].x) / 2; ctx.bezierCurveTo(mx, coords[i].y, mx, coords[i + 1].y, coords[i + 1].x, coords[i + 1].y); } ctx.strokeStyle = isPlat ? '#2563eb' : '#38bdf8'; ctx.lineWidth = 2.4; ctx.shadowColor = '#38bdf8'; ctx.shadowBlur = 9; ctx.stroke(); ctx.restore(); } // Crosshair + Data Nodes + X Labels coords.forEach((pt, i) => { const isH = hoverIdx === i; if (isH) { ctx.save(); ctx.setLineDash([3, 3]); ctx.strokeStyle = '#c084fc'; ctx.lineWidth = 1.2; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(pt.x, padT); ctx.lineTo(pt.x, padT + ph); ctx.stroke(); ctx.restore(); } ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(pt.x, pt.y, isH ? 5 : 3.1, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fillStyle = isH ? '#ffffff' : '#38bdf8'; ctx.fill(); ctx.lineWidth = 1.6; ctx.strokeStyle = isH ? '#7c3aed' : (isPlat ? '#ffffff' : '#060b18'); ctx.stroke(); ctx.fillStyle = isH ? (isPlat ? '#2563eb' : '#38bdf8') : (isPlat ? '#475569' : '#94a3b8'); ctx.font = (isH ? '800' : '700') + ' 8px "JetBrains Mono", monospace'; ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.fillText(pt.data.label, pt.x, h - 8); }); } function bindBentoWealthCanvasEvents(snap) { const donutC = document.getElementById('xbBwDonutCanvas'); if (donutC) { donutC.onclick = (ev) => { const meta = donutC._sliceMeta; if (!meta || !meta.segs || !meta.segs.length) return; const rc = donutC.getBoundingClientRect(); const x = ev.clientX - rc.left - meta.cx; const y = (ev.clientY - rc.top - meta.cy) / (meta.ry / meta.rx); const dist = Math.hypot(x, y); if (dist < meta.rx * meta.innerRatio * 0.85 || dist > meta.rx * 1.25) { selectBentoWealthSlice(-1); return; } let ang = Math.atan2(y, x); if (ang < -Math.PI / 2) ang += Math.PI * 2; const hit = meta.segs.find(s => ang >= s.startAng && ang <= s.endAng); if (hit) selectBentoWealthSlice(hit.idx); }; } const trendC = document.getElementById('xbBwTrendCanvas'); const tipEl = document.getElementById('xbBwTrendInspect'); if (trendC && tipEl) { const inspect = (clientX) => { const coords = trendC._trendCoords || []; if (!coords.length) return; const x = clientX - trendC.getBoundingClientRect().left; let bestIdx = 0, bestDist = Infinity; coords.forEach((pt, idx) => { const d = Math.abs(pt.x - x); if (d < bestDist) { bestDist = d; bestIdx = idx; } }); window._xbBentoWealth.hoverTrendIdx = bestIdx; }; trendC.ontouchmove = e => { if (e.touches && e.touches[0]) inspect(e.touches[0].clientX); }; trendC.onmouseleave = () => { window._xbBentoWealth.hoverTrendIdx = -1; drawBento3DTrendCanvas(snap); }; ['Kirim', 'Send'], ['Beli', 'Buy'], ['Cari', 'Search'] ]; function xbShouldSkipString(str) { const t = String(str || '').trim(); if (!t || t.length < 2) return true; if (/^https?:\/\//i.test(t)) return true; if (/^@[a-zA-Z0-9._-]+$/.test(t)) return true; if (/^(ghp_|github_pat_)/i.test(t)) return true; if (/^(INV|TRF|NEG|MSG|BATCH|NTF)-[A-Z0-9-]+$/i.test(t)) return true; if (/^[A-Z2-7]{16,}$/.test(t)) return true; if (/^[\s\d.,$+\-:%()\/•●○✓✕×▼▲▾➔↗⚡⭐👑🔥🛡️🔒🔓🔑💳🪙🚀👤🔐⚙️📩🔔🛒📋💾🌐☀️🌌⏳📡🎉❌⚠️ℹ️±~RpUSDTIDR]+$/.test(t)) return true; return false; } function xbTranslateText(raw, targetLang = window.currentLang) { if (typeof raw !== 'string') return raw; const leadMatch = raw.match(/^([\s\u00A0]*)/); const trailMatch = raw.match(/([\s\u00A0]*)$/); const lead = leadMatch ? leadMatch[1] : ''; const trail = trailMatch ? trailMatch[1] : ''; const core = raw.trim(); if (xbShouldSkipString(core)) return raw; // Split leading decorative emoji/symbols so "💳 Wallet & Ledger" matches "Wallet & Ledger" const prefMatch = core.match(/^((?:[\p{Emoji_Presentation}\p{Extended_Pictographic}\u2600-\u27BF\uFE0F●○✓✕×★☆▸•━═←→⬅➔▼▲▾↻+]+\s*)+)(.*)$/u); const prefix = prefMatch ? prefMatch[1] : ''; const body = prefMatch ? prefMatch[2].trim() : core; if (!body || xbShouldSkipString(body)) return raw; const isId = (targetLang === 'ID'); const exactMap = isId ? XB_I18N_MAP_EN_TO_ID : XB_I18N_MAP_ID_TO_EN; const bodyLower = body.toLowerCase(); // 1. Exact Match (preserving ALL-CAPS if original body was all uppercase letters) if (exactMap.has(bodyLower)) { let res = exactMap.get(bodyLower); const lettersOnly = body.replace(/[^A-Za-z]/g, ''); if (lettersOnly.length >= 3 && lettersOnly === lettersOnly.toUpperCase()) { res = res.toUpperCase(); } return lead + prefix + res + trail; } // 2. Protect URLs, @handles, Invoices, and Currency amounts before Regex / Phrase pass const stash = []; let work = body.replace(/(https?:\/\/\S+|@[A-Za-z0-9._-]+|\b(?:INV|TRF|NEG|MSG|BATCH)-[A-Z0-9-]+\b|\bghp_[A-Za-z0-9]+\b)/gi, (m) => { const idx = stash.length; stash.push(m); return `__XBK${idx}__`; }); const rxList = isId ? XB_I18N_REGEX_TO_ID : XB_I18N_REGEX_TO_EN; for (let i = 0; i < rxList.length; i++) { work = work.replace(rxList[i][0], rxList[i][1]); } // 3. Multi-word & Safe Word-Boundary Fallback const fbList = isId ? XB_I18N_FALLBACK_TO_ID : XB_I18N_FALLBACK_TO_EN; for (let i = 0; i < fbList.length; i++) { const [src, dst] = fbList[i]; const escSrc = src.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&'); const rgx = new RegExp(`(? { const letters = m.replace(/[^A-Za-z]/g, ''); if (letters.length >= 3 && letters === letters.toUpperCase()) return dst.toUpperCase(); if (m[0] && m[0] === m[0].toLowerCase() && dst[0]) return dst[0].toLowerCase() + dst.slice(1); return dst; }); } // Restore protected tokens work = work.replace(/__XBK(\d+)__/g, (_, i) => stash[Number(i)] || ''); return lead + prefix + work + trail; } const _xbI18nTextMeta = new WeakMap(); const _xbI18nAttrMeta = new WeakMap(); const _xbSkipTags = new Set(['SCRIPT', 'STYLE', 'NOSCRIPT', 'SVG', 'CANVAS', 'TEXTAREA']); let _xbI18nMutating = false; function xbIsProtectedElement(el) { if (!el || el.nodeType !== 1) return false; if (_xbSkipTags.has(el.tagName)) return true; if (el.closest('[data-no-i18n="1"], .xb-msg-bub, #web2faCode, #admSlotPatVal, .cred-field-row .val')) return true; if (el.id && /^pat_tok_disp_/.test(el.id)) return true; return false; } function xbTranslateNodeTree(rootNode) { if (!rootNode) return; _xbI18nMutating = true; try { const lang = window.currentLang || 'EN'; const walker = document.createTreeWalker(rootNode, NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT | NodeFilter.SHOW_ELEMENT, null); let node = rootNode; while (node) { if (node.nodeType === 1) { if (!xbIsProtectedElement(node)) { const attrs = ['placeholder', 'title']; let map = _xbI18nAttrMeta.get(node); attrs.forEach(at => { if (!node.hasAttribute(at)) return; const curVal = node.getAttribute(at) || ''; if (!curVal) return; if (!map) { map = {}; _xbI18nAttrMeta.set(node, map); } if (!map[at] || curVal !== map[at].applied) { map[at] = { orig: curVal, applied: curVal }; } const nextVal = xbTranslateText(map[at].orig, lang); if (nextVal !== curVal) { map[at].applied = nextVal; node.setAttribute(at, nextVal); } }); } } else if (node.nodeType === 3) { const parent = node.parentElement; if (parent && !xbIsProtectedElement(parent)) { const cur = node.nodeValue || ''; if (cur.trim().length >= 2) { let meta = _xbI18nTextMeta.get(node); if (!meta || cur !== meta.applied) { meta = { orig: cur, applied: cur }; _xbI18nTextMeta.set(node, meta); } const next = xbTranslateText(meta.orig, lang); if (next !== cur) { meta.applied = next; node.nodeValue = next; } } } } node = walker.nextNode(); } } finally { _xbI18nMutating = false; } } function updateLangSwitchUI() { const lang = window.currentLang || 'EN'; document.documentElement.setAttribute('lang', lang === 'ID' ? 'id' : 'en'); document.querySelectorAll('.xb-lang-btn').forEach(btn => { btn.classList.toggle('active', btn.getAttribute('data-lang') === lang); }); } function setAppLanguage(langCode) { const nextLang = String(langCode || 'EN').toUpperCase() === 'ID' ? 'ID' : 'EN'; window.currentLang = nextLang; localStorage.setItem('xb_lang_mode', nextLang); updateLangSwitchUI(); xbTranslateNodeTree(document.body); if (typeof showToast === 'function') { showToast(nextLang === 'ID' ? 'Bahasa Indonesia diaktifkan!' : 'English language enabled!'); } } // Hook showToast, confirm, and prompt so popups never leak the wrong language if (typeof window.showToast === 'function' && !window._xbToastI18nHooked) { window._xbToastI18nHooked = true; const _origShowToast = window.showToast; window.showToast = function (msg) { return _origShowToast(xbTranslateText(String(msg || ''), window.currentLang)); }; } if (!window._xbDialogI18nHooked) { window._xbDialogI18nHooked = true; const _origConfirm = window.confirm.bind(window); const _origPrompt = window.prompt.bind(window); window.confirm = function (msg) { return _origConfirm(xbTranslateText(String(msg || ''), window.currentLang)); }; window.prompt = function (msg, defVal) { return _origPrompt(xbTranslateText(String(msg || ''), window.currentLang), defVal); }; } // Real-time MutationObserver for all dynamic renders, modals & live refreshes let _xbI18nRaf = null; const _xbI18nObserver = new MutationObserver(() => { if (_xbI18nMutating || _xbI18nRaf) return; _xbI18nRaf = requestAnimationFrame(() => { _xbI18nRaf = null; xbTranslateNodeTree(document.body); }); }); // Initial Render & Auth Gate Check checkInitialAuthGate(); switchTab('home'); setInterval(updateAuthPortalTickers, 5000); updateLangSwitchUI(); xbTranslateNodeTree(document.body); _xbI18nObserver.observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true, characterData: true });
